18 14:46:47     Q. And what was the purpose of including those in the
19 14:46:50        WPSs in particular?
20 14:46:54     A. Well, it is a regular procedure in all the projects,
21 14:46:58        in all the subcontract administration, it's another
22 14:47:01        subcontract administrative procedure.
23 14:47:03            The WPS, the progress payment, is always made out
24 14:47:07        of quantities, multiplied by unit prices, maybe some
25 14:47:11        indirect lump sum, plus any variation, additional
                                     100
 1 14:47:16        modification or additional works that are out are not
 2 14:47:19        included in the BOQ for several reasons, plus the back
 3 14:47:24        charges that need to be deducted according to the
 4 14:47:27        subcontract provisions whenever something is paid on
 5 14:47:30        behalf of, or something is injected to, or something
 6 14:47:34        is to fix a defect, from Down Town, for instance,
 7 14:47:42        fixed by the subcontractor.
 8 14:47:43            So this is typically the structure of any WPS, any
